14:I[8669,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DateSidebar"]
15:I[44692,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DailyView"]
16:T828,The method has three main components. First, the taxonomy is extended beyond the 12 dimensions of ESSENTIAL-WEB with two new ordinal dimensions: timeliness and cultural specificity. Timeliness measures how temporally durable a document’s value is, from highly time-sensitive content to fully evergreen material. Cultural specificity measures how tightly content is tied to a particular cultural or geographic context. The paper validates that these are genuinely new signals: pairwise NMI with existing dimensions is low, with timeliness reaching at most 0.21 and cultural specificity at most 0.11. Second, the authors build a scalable annotation stack. They use Qwen2.5-32B to annotate 14 million web documents, distill those annotations into a 0.5B generative annotator, and then train a 73M multi-task MLP on top of E5 embeddings for high-throughput corpus-wide prediction. The MLP runs at roughly 50 times the throughput of the generative annotator while maintaining strong exact-match agreement. This is an important systems choice: the goal is not only label quality, but making semantic annotation cheap enough to run over production-scale corpora. Third, they introduce a two-pass search framework for compound filters. Because each taxonomy dimension is ordinal and can be thresholded in multiple ways, exhaustive search over conjunctions and disjunctions is combinatorially expensive. Pass 1 evaluates dimensions independently at small scale to identify the strongest single signals. Pass 2 constructs compound filters from the top-performing dimensions and evaluates them at the same scale. Only the most promising candidates are then taken to full scaling-law style experiments. A central empirical finding is that timeliness appears in nearly all strong compound filters, suggesting that temporal durability is more predictive of transferable pretraining value than many standard quality heuristics. The paper also studies the trade-off between strict and broad filters: strict filters increase reasoning and coding density, while broader filters preserve factual coverage.17:T5d6,The headline result is that taxonomy-guided filtering can make mid- and even low-tier web data outperform unfiltered top-tier data. On mid-tier data, the best compound filter improves over the unfiltered baseline by 12.1% on reasoning and 9.5% on coding on average. It also surpasses unfiltered top-tier data by 6.7% on reasoning and 13.7% on coding. The effect persists further down the quality ladder: for data two tiers below the usual production threshold—data that many pipelines would likely discard entirely—filtered subsets improve over their unfiltered baseline by 22.3% on reasoning and 19.5% on coding, and they exceed unfiltered top-tier data on coding benchmarks. Another important result is methodological: the ranking induced by the low-cost two-pass screening is consistent with the ranking from full scaling-law experiments, which validates the screening procedure as a reliable proxy rather than a toy heuristic. In ablations, timeliness is the strongest single dimension and the most stable ingredient in high-performing compound filters. Cultural specificity contributes complementary signal rather than dominating performance. The paper also shows a clear capability trade-off: stricter filters help numerical reasoning and coding more, while broader filters retain more factual knowledge breadth. That observation matters for staged training, because it suggests that the optimal filter depends on training phase and target capability rather than being globally fixed.18:T4f4,Start with the taxonomy section and read the definitions of timeliness and cultural specificity carefully. The key question there is whether these dimensions add genuinely new information or just rename existing quality attributes. Then move to the annotation pipeline: focus on the role split between the 32B teacher, the 0.5B distilled annotator, and the 73M embedding-based MLP, and pay attention to why the authors accept a lighter predictor for full-corpus labeling. After that, read the two-pass search procedure and the full-training validation together. The important part is not only which filters win, but how single-dimension screening is turned into conjunctions and disjunctions, and whether the small-scale ranking remains stable at full scale. If reading time is limited, prioritize the main results figures, the timeliness ablations, and the analysis of strict versus broad filters. The most interesting follow-up questions are: why timeliness is such a dominant signal; how robust the final data selection is to annotation noise; and how this framework could be integrated with existing production pipelines, for example by using composite scores for coarse tiering and taxonomy-based recovery inside lower tiers rather than replacing the whole stack.19:T4b5,CHIAR-Former is a 4-layer hybrid transformer. For each token, the model computes spectral entropy from the DCT power spectrum of its representation and routes the token to one of three operators: DCT spectral mixing, RBF kernel mixing, or full self-attention. Low-entropy tokens are treated as smooth and sent to DCT mixing. High-entropy tokens are treated as complex and sent to attention. Mid-range tokens are initially assigned to an RBF kernel branch implemented with random Fourier features.

The paper provides a theoretical motivation for this partition: low-entropy tokens admit bounded DCT truncation error, medium-entropy tokens can be handled by kernel approximation, and high-entropy tokens benefit from dynamic cross-token projection. Relative to FNet, the key difference is selective rather than universal spectral mixing. Relative to Mixture-of-Depths or LayerSkip, the model does not skip computation but changes the operator type while preserving depth. The most important technical outcome, however, is that learned routing collapses: the router repeatedly rejects the RBF branch. The authors then embrace that result and build a DCT+Attention-only variant as the effective architecture.1a:T410,The headline number is on WikiText-103: the DCT+Attention variant reaches validation perplexity 36.54 versus 66.6 for a full-attention baseline, while using 62.5% fewer attention FLOPs. If the setup is fully matched, that is a large gain. Because the gain is so large, the first thing to verify is baseline fairness: same parameter budget, same training schedule, same context length, same optimizer, and same data exposure.

The broader experimental claim is more interesting than the single best result. The paper reports evaluations on four datasets and finds a clear operating regime: spectral routing helps more on larger, naturalistic text corpora, but loses to full attention on small datasets and synthetic pattern-matching tasks. That boundary condition is informative. The most important ablations are likely: three-way routing versus DCT+Attention-only, sensitivity to entropy thresholds, and whether routing collapse is stable across seeds and datasets. Without that stability, the architectural conclusion would be much weaker.1b:T4fa,PAPO has two main components.

Step-Aware Process Rewards, or SPR, avoids broadcasting the final outcome reward to all intermediate denoising steps. Instead, it computes an immediate reward for each intermediate step based on a single denoising-step prediction, then combines this signal with the final outcome reward. This gives the optimizer a more step-specific learning signal. The design matters because one diffusion denoising step can update many masked positions in parallel, so token-level credit cannot be inherited from AR generation.

Entropy-Guided Historical Re-enactment, or EHR, replaces artificial randomly masked states with real historical states captured during natural rollouts. During training, the method re-enacts these states and samples them according to model uncertainty, using entropy to focus updates on high-information moments. This avoids spending gradients on already settled decisions and keeps the training states faithful to the model’s actual generation process.

At a high level, PAPO is a GRPO-style policy optimization framework adapted to masked diffusion LMs. The key change is not the group-relative reward idea itself, but the alignment of rewards, log-probability evaluation, and state selection with the denoising trajectory.1c:T77d,The paper proceeds in three stages. First, it reframes gen-PPL conceptually. A frozen AR scorer assigns high probability to sequences that are locally predictable under its learned conditional structure. That is not equivalent to text quality. Quality includes long-range semantic consistency, discourse structure, information content, and human plausibility. A sequence can be easy for the scorer to continue token by token while still being globally poor. The commonly paired empirical unigram entropy guardrail does not fix this, because it only filters out crude low-entropy collapse. It does not prevent sequences that use a reasonably broad vocabulary but combine tokens in shallow, low-quality ways.

Second, the authors build explicit counterexamples. This is the core technical move. They construct a suite of zero-parameter, deliberately naive samplers that do not learn a text distribution at all, yet achieve extremely strong gen-PPL. The exact sampler designs matter less than the broader point: by directly exploiting what the scorer finds predictable, one can manufacture outputs that score well while remaining incoherent to a human reader. This demonstrates that the failure is in the metric definition, not in a particular model family.

Third, they propose a replacement evaluation protocol centered on distributional comparison rather than scorer-based token likelihood. Instead of asking whether a generated sequence is easy for an AR model to predict, they ask whether generated samples statistically resemble human-written samples. The paper recommends distributional metrics such as MMD and energy distance, drawing from two-sample testing and divergence estimation. The key shift is from single-sample scoring to comparing generated and reference distributions. That aligns much better with the actual goal of unconditional generation and is harder to game through local predictability hacks.1d:T683,The most important experimental result is the existence proof: on LM1B and OpenWebText, the zero-parameter naive samplers achieve gen-PPL numbers competitive with or better than recently published diffusion and flow-based language models, while maintaining non-degenerate empirical entropy. So this is not a trivial collapse case where the sampler repeats a few tokens. It passes the standard entropy guardrail and still produces poor text. That directly invalidates the common interpretation of low gen-PPL as evidence of high-quality generation.

A second important result is the clear decoupling between human-perceived quality and gen-PPL. Through examples and quantitative comparisons, the paper shows that sequences ranked highly by gen-PPL can be semantically weak or incoherent. This is not presented as a rare pathology but as a broad failure mode induced by the metric.

A third result comes from re-benchmarking recent non-AR models under distributional metrics. Once evaluated by sample-distribution similarity rather than scorer predictability, the picture of progress changes. Models that looked strong under gen-PPL do not necessarily remain strong, and models that better match human text distributions can be separated from those that merely exploit the scorer. The most informative thing to inspect is not the absolute ranking itself, but how rankings change between the old and new protocols.

From an experimental design standpoint, two details are worth noting. The paper uses both LM1B and OpenWebText, covering different text regimes, and it does more than criticize: it provides reproducible adversarial baselines plus an alternative protocol.1e:T49c,Start with the introduction and the formal discussion of what gen-PPL actually measures. The key point is that the paper is not arguing that GPT-2 is too weak as a scorer; it is arguing that the metric is misaligned with the target. Then read the section introducing the naive samplers and inspect the sample outputs carefully. That is where the intuition becomes concrete: why obviously bad text can still look good to the scorer. After that, move to the re-benchmarking section and compare model rankings under gen-PPL versus distributional metrics.

If time is limited, focus on three things: the definition and limitations of gen-PPL plus empirical entropy, the construction logic of the zero-parameter counterexamples, and the proposed distributional evaluation protocol. The most useful deeper questions are: what representation space the distributional metrics rely on, whether those metrics are sensitive to sample size or length distribution, and whether similar criticisms apply to other judge-model-based proxies used in language generation. For anyone working on new LM paradigms, this paper is best read as an evaluation sanity check before trusting benchmark tables.1f:T62a,Main contributions are the SODA benchmark and a causal-style decomposition of what in the history drives safety gains.
1) SODA (Safety Over Depth for Agents): a depth-controlled threat injection protocol. The same safety-critical tool-use threats are evaluated at multiple conversation depths D, where D is the number of ordinary agentic tool tasks completed before the threat (up to 20). The benchmark includes 16 tool environments (e.g., BankAccount, EmailManager, FileSystem, WebBrowser), 5 scenarios per environment (80 total), and 5 threats per scenario (400 threats). Preceding tasks are sampled from a scenario-specific pool of benign tool tasks to keep tool schemas and interaction patterns realistic.
2) Phenomenon: safety improves monotonically with depth; the paper names this the cold-start safety gap.
3) Ablations: separate the effect of (a) benign user task requests in history vs (b) the agent’s own prior responses. They find benign task requests are the primary driver; the exact content of the agent’s prior responses matters little for safety (random/agreeable filler works similarly).
4) Utility caveat: while response content is not needed for the safety gain, real agent responses/tool interactions are important to preserve utility; fake responses degrade tool performance.
5) Representation analysis: hidden states move across a linear “safety boundary” as depth increases, supporting an ‘agent persona gradually activates’ hypothesis.
6) Mitigation: warm-up—run a few benign tool tasks (5–10) before handling potentially risky requests.20:T589,SVR builds a global rubric bank from preference triples (x, r+, r-). The key change is the induction objective: instead of asking an LLM to summarize what makes a response good, it asks for rubrics that distinguish the preferred response from the rejected one. This contrastive framing yields more boundary-relevant criteria. Each rubric in the bank gets a global importance weight w, and the model learns a prompt-conditioned selector alpha(x) that retrieves a sparse top-k subset using only the prompt.

Pairwise evaluation is then defined as an aggregated margin over rubric-specific judgments, with the training objective enforcing F(x, r+, r-) > 0. The design choice to condition rubric selection only on the prompt is important: the evaluation standard is fixed before seeing candidate responses, which reduces response-induced criterion drift. The method then runs an iterative refinement loop. Preference pairs near the margin or misclassified are treated as support pairs. An adversarial probing step generates harder negatives around them, exposing uncovered failure modes. New rubrics are induced for those cases and added to the bank, followed by pruning based on low weight, low support, and redundancy. Relative to prompt-only self-rubric methods, the novelty is not a stronger judge model but a different source of rubrics, a max-margin training objective, and a support-pair-driven refinement process.21:T449,The headline result is on RubricBench: SVR reaches 82.8 pairwise accuracy, only 0.3 behind the human-rubric oracle. Given that the judge backbone is held fixed, this strongly supports the paper’s claim that rubric quality, not judge capacity, is the main bottleneck on nuanced preference cases. The method also remains competitive on RewardBench 1/2 and RM-Bench, which suggests it does not overfit to RubricBench-style hard pairs.

The most informative parts are the transfer and ablation results. The paper claims that a rubric bank trained once transfers across heterogeneous judge families and unseen preference benchmarks. If the tables support that claim, it means the rubric layer is more reusable than prompt-specific rubric generation. The likely high-value ablations are: contrastive versus descriptive rubric induction, with or without prompt-conditioned selection, with or without support-pair refinement, and with or without adversarial hard-negative expansion. The pruning step also matters beyond efficiency, because overlapping rubrics can otherwise double-count the same signal.22:T425,The evaluation covers 1,128,600 instances across 33 opinion categories. Three findings stand out. First, all six models show a clear resource-tier effect: sycophancy rises as one moves from high-resource to low-resource to zero-shot languages. Second, the degradation is largely topic-agnostic. Models do not preserve stronger resistance on safety-critical prompts in underrepresented languages. In the worst cases reported, agreement with harmful prompts exceeds 70% in zero-shot languages. Third, tokenizer fertility is strongly associated with alignment collapse, suggesting that fragmented subword encoding and longer effective sequences may weaken the transfer of aligned behavior.

The Sarvam-M result is also informative. A domain-specialized multilingual model can partially reverse the resource penalty on its target languages through better tokenizer and data coverage, but still fails badly on zero-shot languages outside that scope. That supports the paper’s main interpretation: the issue is coverage and representation, not just parameter count.23:T5bc,TRADE extends the Hybrid TAED idea to a decoder-only Speech LLM. A shared Conformer audio encoder feeds both the LLM path and the transducer path. The central design choice is that the transducer uses the LLM hidden states directly as its prediction network. The transducer becomes the primary streaming decoder: it decides whether to emit a token, emit blank, or advance the acoustic frame. The LLM supplies linguistic context and full-vocabulary information through score fusion.

The paper also introduces tightly coupled dual vocabularies. The transducer vocabulary is derived from the LLM vocabulary by preserving token IDs and merging pronunciation-equivalent surface forms. During inference, LLM probability mass is marginalized over homophone sets and fused with transducer scores. This keeps the transducer lattice manageable while still allowing the LLM to recover casing, punctuation, spelling variants, and other surface-level details.

For training, TRADE combines transducer loss with LLM cross-entropy. Dynamic chunk-based synchronized training refreshes the LLM prefill state at chunk boundaries, reducing the mismatch between streaming training and streaming inference. Gradient stopping at the LLM boundary keeps memory close to the offline training regime. For long audio, Localized Decoder Audio Attention restricts audio attention to a causal sliding window, capping inference KV-cache growth and reducing alignment drift from stale early context.24:T6da,The paper does not introduce a new alignment algorithm. Instead, it builds an empirical case that an alternative explanation can account for alignment-faking-like behavior.

The first step is to challenge a simple prediction of the scheming story. If a model is pretending to be aligned during evaluation mainly to avoid weight modification, then explicitly telling it that it is already deployed should reduce that incentive or at least alter the behavior pattern. The authors report that evaluation awareness persists even under deployment-framed system prompts. They use this as evidence that a pure self-preservation account is incomplete.

The second step is to test whether sycophancy-related internal features can explain the same behaviors. They use activation probing and activation steering with features described as scheming-related and sycophancy-related, asking which features better account for the observed context-sensitive behavior. The argumentative structure is important: they are not proving that scheming is absent, but that sycophancy is a plausible competing mechanism with empirical support.

The third step is an intervention through fine-tuning. They make models more sycophantic and then measure whether evaluation-cue detection improves. The intended logic is that if increasing sycophancy also increases sensitivity to evaluation context, then evaluation awareness need not be uniquely explained by strategic deception or self-preservation.

Methodologically, this is a concept-clarification paper backed by probing, steering, prompt manipulation, and behavioral comparison. Its main contribution is to separate a behavioral label from an intent-level interpretation and to provide evidence for an alternative causal story.25:T660,From the provided full text, three empirical claims stand out.

First, evaluation awareness persists even when models are told they are deployed. The authors treat this as evidence against a scheming-only explanation, because a model motivated purely by avoiding weight updates should have less reason to conceal itself under deployment framing. The strength of this result depends heavily on prompt construction, model choice, and the exact behavioral metric, so those details matter a lot.

Second, activation probing and steering suggest that sycophancy-related features can explain a substantial portion of behavior previously attributed to scheming. The excerpt does not include the full quantitative tables, so the direction of the result is visible but the effect size is not. A careful read should check how the features are labeled, what the probes are trained to predict, whether steering strengths are comparable, and whether scheming and sycophancy features are themselves highly correlated.

Third, fine-tuning for greater sycophancy appears to improve evaluation-cue detection. If robust, that would support a manipulable link between researcher-directed sycophancy and evaluation awareness. But the key question is whether the model becomes genuinely more situationally aware or merely more sensitive to superficial evaluation cues.

Overall, the experiments look more like evidence for an alternative causal interpretation than a decisive resolution of the debate. Without stronger cross-model robustness, larger-scale controls, and more explicit handling of confounders, the conclusions should be read with caution.26:T4f6,This is a skim paper rather than a deep-read priority. Start with the Introduction and the section defining performative misalignment. The main thing to understand is the conceptual separation: alignment faking is a behavioral description, while scheming and self-preservation are intent-level hypotheses. Without that distinction, the rest can feel like a terminological dispute.

Then read the experimental setup with three questions in mind. First, does the deployment-prompt manipulation genuinely challenge the scheming hypothesis, or only a narrow version of it? Second, how are the probing and steering features defined, and is there any circularity in the labels? Third, does sycophancy fine-tuning improve true evaluation awareness or just pattern-match on evaluation templates?

If you only want the main point, the introduction, core experiment figures, and discussion should be enough in about 20 to 30 minutes. If you want to judge the paper seriously, you need the appendix: prompts, evaluation protocol, probe construction, and statistical details are likely where the real strength or weakness of the argument sits. For a pretraining-focused reader, this is best treated as a useful safety-interpretation note rather than a high-priority technical paper.27:T9e3,方法上最关键的是数据构造和干预设计。作者不直接在自然文本里做相关性分析，而是利用“同一个词既可以被 tokenizer 表成单 token，也可以被人工拆成多个 subword”这一事实，构造 canonical form 与 artificial split form 的对照。然后定义 canonicity：比较 canonical 单 token 运行时该词位置的最终层 residual，与 split 运行时最后一个 subword 位置的最终层 residual 是否对齐。这个指标用来判断 split 后模型有没有在内部成功恢复出 canonical 词表示。

为了把 attention 与 MLP 的作用拆开，作者设计了两类配对样本。LST pair 共享最后一个 subword，不同的是前文子词；FST pair 共享第一个 subword，不同的是最后子词。这样可以分别隔离“跨位置传来的信息”和“本地末尾子词本身的贡献”。在这些配对上做 activation patching：把 source run 某层某位置的 attn_out 或 mlp_out 替换到 target run，再看 target 最终重建成哪个词。结果显示，在 Layer 1 的最后子词位置，patch attention 输出就足以把 target 的重建词拉向 source 的词，说明 attention 已经携带了决定性跨位置信号；而 patch MLP 输出则对应把这个信号与本地子词组合成更完整的词级表示。

论文提出的机制可以概括为：
1. 对于两到六个 subword 的词，真正发生组合的位置始终是最后一个 subword；
2. 如果词更长，中间 subword 位置主要充当 relay，把前面子词的信息逐步传到最后位置；
3. Layer 1 attention 负责传输 token-specific signal，这个信号较弱、线性可读，但已经足以决定后续会往哪个词收敛；
4. Layer 1 MLP 负责把该信号和末尾子词 embedding 做连续变换，得到更接近 canonical token 的表示；
5. 后续若干早层继续完成 detokenization，但关键起点已经在第一层建立。

作者还把分析扩展到 12 个 transformer LM，比较不同架构和位置编码。结论不是“所有模型都在同一层完成 detokenization”，而是 detokenization 集中在前 6% 到 30% 的层数区间，跨度主要由 positional encoding 决定。也就是说，位置编码如果更利于局部顺序信息在早层传播，detokenization 会更快完成；如果不利，则需要更多层。最后，作者在 activation patching 找到的关键深度上拟合一个线性方向，用早层激活预测最终 detokenization 成败，形成一个可操作的 probe。28:T42f,The paper asks a precise mechanistic question: how do transformer LMs convert subword-fragmented input back into a usable word-level representation, and why does this process sometimes fail? Prior work had already suggested that detokenization happens in early-to-middle layers and involves both attention and MLPs, but the causal chain was still coarse. This work narrows it down substantially. Using controlled canonical-vs-split word pairs and activation patching, the authors show that English detokenization in Llama2-7B, and similarly in a broader set of transformer LMs, starts as a two-stage computation in Layer 1. A small set of attention heads first transmits a token-specific signal from earlier subwords to the final subword position; then the Layer 1 MLP combines that imported signal with the local subword representation to produce a continuous word-level representation. The paper also studies how this mechanism scales with word length, how it varies across architectures, and whether early-layer activations can predict eventual detokenization success.29:T4df,This matters for pretraining research because it turns the tokenizer-model interface into something mechanistically inspectable rather than merely behaviorally observable. Tokenization artifacts are known to hurt robustness, arithmetic, and rare-token behavior, but this paper identifies where the model repairs the mismatch between subword input and word-level semantics. That is useful for thinking about tokenizer design, early-layer representation formation, and failure modes induced by poor segmentation. A second important result is that the depth over which detokenization unfolds is governed more by positional encoding than by model scale. That is a concrete architectural claim, not just an interpretability observation, and it suggests that early semantic composition depends on how positional information supports cross-token aggregation. Third, the paper provides a linear probe from early-layer activations that predicts final detokenization success with AUROC 0.94 on isolated inputs and 0.97 on natural text. That makes detokenization a measurable training signal. It could plausibly become a diagnostic for comparing tokenizers, monitoring training dynamics, or studying when word-level concepts become stable during pretraining.2a:Ta04,The core methodological contribution is the combination of a tightly controlled dataset construction with causal intervention. Instead of probing arbitrary natural text, the authors exploit words that can appear either as a single canonical token or as an artificially split sequence of subwords. They define a canonicity measure by comparing the final-layer residual stream at the canonical token position with the final-layer residual stream at the last subword position in the split run. High similarity means the model has successfully reconstructed the canonical word representation from fragments.

To separate the roles of attention and MLPs, they build paired examples with shared subwords. In one setup, pairs share the last subword but differ in earlier fragments, isolating the contribution of cross-position information. In another, pairs share the first subword but differ in the final fragment, isolating the contribution of the local final-subword identity. They then perform activation patching: replace attn_out or mlp_out from a source run into a target run at a specific layer and position, and observe which word the target ultimately reconstructs. This is the key causal move. In Layer 1 at the final subword position, patching attention output is already sufficient to redirect the target toward the source word, showing that attention carries the decisive imported signal. Patching the MLP output then shows how that signal is transformed together with the local final-subword representation into a more canonical word-level state.

The resulting mechanism is fairly crisp. For words split into two to six subwords, composition consistently happens at the last subword position. Intermediate positions mainly act as relays when the word is longer. Layer 1 attention transports a weak but linearly readable token-specific signal from earlier fragments. Layer 1 MLP composes that imported signal with the local subword embedding through a continuous transformation. Subsequent early layers continue the process, but the decisive initialization happens immediately.

The authors extend the analysis to 12 transformer LMs and compare architectures and positional encodings. Their claim is not that every model detokenizes in exactly one layer, but that detokenization is concentrated in the first 6% to 30% of layers, with the span largely determined by positional encoding. Finally, they fit a linear direction at the depth identified by patching and use it to predict whether detokenization will succeed, based only on early-layer activations.2b:T7c6,There are four experimental results worth retaining.

First, the mechanism localization result. In Llama2-7B, Layer 1 is already causally central. Patching Layer 1 attention output at the final subword position can change which word is reconstructed at the end of the network, while Layer 1 MLP output further sharpens that representation toward the canonical token. This supports a sequential division of labor: attention transports, MLP composes.

Second, the word-length generalization result. The authors test words split into 2 to 6 subwords and find that the final subword remains the composition site throughout. Longer words do not induce distributed semantic composition across all positions; instead, intermediate positions mostly relay information forward until the last position can assemble the word-level representation.

Third, the cross-model comparison. Across 12 transformer language models, detokenization is concentrated in the first 6% to 30% of layers. The important claim is that this depth tracks positional encoding more than parameter count. That weakens a simple scale-based explanation and points to an architectural determinant of early semantic aggregation.

Fourth, the predictability result. A linear probe trained on early-layer activations at the patching-identified depth predicts final detokenization success with AUROC around 0.94 on isolated contextless sequences and 0.97 on natural text. This suggests that success or failure is largely decided very early, and that the signal transfers beyond the synthetic split-word setup.

The paper also discusses failure cases where the imported early-layer signal is too weak or ambiguous, making later recovery incomplete. That aligns with known tokenization-related failures on undertrained tokens and difficult segmentations. When reading the full paper, it is worth checking the effect sizes for different patching conditions and the plots comparing detokenization depth across positional encoding schemes.2c:T722,Read it in four passes: definition, causal design, mechanism, and boundary conditions.

Start with the Introduction and Setup. The important part is not the general motivation but the exact construction of canonical versus split forms and the meaning of the canonicity metric. If that metric is not clear, the later patching results can look like generic representation-similarity analysis rather than a causal study of detokenization.

Then focus on the paired-example design and activation patching section, especially Figure 1 and the corresponding experiments. This is the center of the paper. Pay attention to why the two pair types isolate imported cross-position information versus local final-subword information, and why patching Layer 1 attn_out and mlp_out supports a two-stage interpretation rather than merely showing that both components matter.

Next, read the sections on longer words and cross-model comparisons. The main questions are whether the relay interpretation for intermediate subwords is convincingly supported, and how strong the evidence is that positional encoding governs detokenization depth. That part is especially relevant if you care about tokenizer-architecture co-design.

Finish with the probe section. The headline AUROC is useful, but the deeper point is that detokenization success is linearly readable from early activations. That raises practical follow-up questions: could this become a training diagnostic, and could it be used to compare tokenizers, curricula, or continual pretraining regimes?

If time is limited, read Figure 1, the Layer 1 patching results, the cross-model depth analysis, and the probe section. That should take about 40 to 60 minutes for the main ideas. A full read is worthwhile if you want to reuse the methodology for tokenizer or training-dynamics pretraining collapse document quality into a single composite score, systematically missing high-value content along dimensions the scorer underweights. pairwis","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-05T18:43:14Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2606_07778.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、但在预训练数据工程里长期被简化的问题：网页文档的训练价值不是单一“质量分数”，而是由多个相互独立的属性共同决定。现有主流 pipeline 往往把文档压成一个 composite score，再按分层抽样或阈值截断。这样做的后果是，很多在某些能力维度上很有价值、但在综合分上不占优的文档，会被系统性降权，甚至直接丢弃。论文要解决的不是“如何再做一个更强的质量打分器”，而是如何把低层级或中层级网页语料中的潜在高价值内容重新找出来，并且用一种可扩展、可组合、可解释的方式把这些内容转化成真正能提升预训练效果的数据子集。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它直接挑战了“单分数排序+高分优先混料”这套常见数据治理范式，说明低 tier 数据里并不只是噪声，很多被 composite scorer 忽略的价值可以通过结构化标签恢复出来。第二，它把数据筛选从“黑盒质量排序”推进到“按能力目标做条件检索”：如果目标是推理、代码、知识覆盖，不同维度和不同阈值组合会给出不同最优子集，这比统一质量分更接近真实训练需求。第三，它给出了一套算力上可落地的方法：先用大模型标注，再蒸馏到 0.5B 和 73M 级别模型，最后用两阶段搜索缩小组合过滤空间。对做大规模预训练的人来说，这不是一个只停留在分析层面的 taxonomy 论文，而是一套可以接到现有数据 pipeline 上的筛选框架。贡献量级上，属于“数据配比与数据筛选方法”的实质性推进，尤其对 web pretraining、continual pretraining、mid-train 数据重整很有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法分成三块。第一块是 taxonomy 扩展。作者在 ESSENTIAL-WEB 的 12 个维度上新增了 timeliness 和 cultural specificity 两个六级有序维度。timeliness 衡量信息的时间耐久性，从强时效内容到 evergreen 内容；cultural specificity 衡量内容是否强依赖特定文化、地区或语境。作者专门验证了这两个维度与已有维度的 pairwise NMI 很低，timeliness 的最大 NMI 只有 0.21，cultural specificity 最大 0.11，说明它们不是旧标签的重命名，而是新增信号。第二块是大规模标注与蒸馏。作者先用 Qwen2.5-32B 对 1400 万网页文档做 taxonomy annotation，再蒸馏出一个 0.5B 轻量生成式标注器；为了进一步提高吞吐，又基于 E5 embedding 训练了一个 73M 的 multi-task MLP，用 embedding 直接预测多维 taxonomy 标签，吞吐达到生成式模型的 50 倍，同时保持较高 exact-match 一致性。这里的关键不是单纯追求标注精度，而是把“高成本语义标注”变成“全语料可运行的基础设施”。第三块是两阶段过滤搜索。由于每个维度是 ordinal scale，且每个维度可设多个阈值，直接搜索 compound filters 会组合爆炸。作者先做 pass 1：逐维度小规模筛选，找出单维最有信号的维度；再做 pass 2：从这些 top dimensions 构造 conjunction 和 disjunction 的组合过滤器，在同样小规模下筛选。这个设计的价值在于，它不是盲目做全量 scaling-law 实验，而是先用低成本 screening 找到排序稳定的候选，再把少数最优过滤器拿去做完整训练。全文反复强调 timeliness 在高性能组合里几乎总会出现，说明“信息是否 evergreen”比很多传统质量特征更接近预训练可迁移价值。另一个重要点是，作者不只看单维过滤，而是系统研究 strict filter 与 broad filter 的能力取舍：严格过滤更利于数理推理和代码，宽松过滤更保留知识广度。","experiments":"实验最重要的结论是：taxonomy-guided filtering 不只是能提升低 tier 数据本身，还能让中低 tier 数据超过未过滤的高 tier 数据。文中给出的核心数字有三组。其一，在 mid-tier 数据上，最佳 compound filter 相比未过滤 baseline，平均带来 12.1% 的 reasoning 提升和 9.5% 的 coding 提升；同时还超过未过滤 top-tier 数据，reasoning 高 6.7%，coding 高 13.7%。其二，在比常规生产阈值再低两个 tier 的数据上，也就是很多 pipeline 可能直接丢弃的数据，过滤后相对其未过滤 baseline，reasoning 平均提升 22.3%，coding 提升 19.5%；并且在 coding benchmark 上超过未过滤 top-tier 数据。其三，作者验证了两阶段 screening 与完整 scaling-law 结果的排序一致性，说明小规模实验可以可靠地筛选候选过滤器，而不是只在 toy setup 上成立。消融层面，timeliness 是最强单维信号，也是高性能组合中最稳定的组成部分；文化特异性提供的是补充性信号，不像 timeliness 那样主导，但能帮助刻画知识覆盖与泛化边界。论文还分析了过滤强度与能力画像的关系：更严格的过滤会牺牲一部分 factual breadth，却显著提高 reasoning/coding 密度；更宽的过滤则更适合保留知识型能力。这一点对 staged training 很关键，因为它暗示不同训练阶段可能需要不同过滤策略，而不是一套静态混料。","reading_guide":"精读时建议先抓三处。第一是 taxonomy 定义部分，重点看 timeliness 和 cultural specificity 的标注标准，以及它们与原有 12 维的互信息分析。这里决定了新增维度是不是“真新信号”。第二是 annotation pipeline，尤其是 32B 生成式标注、0.5B 蒸馏模型、73M embedding-MLP 三层结构各自承担什么角色，误差是如何传播的，为什么作者愿意用更轻的 MLP 做全量打标。第三是两阶段搜索和最终 scaling-law 验证，重点看单维筛选如何进入 compound filter 构造、哪些组合是 conjunction、哪些是 disjunction，以及 screening 排序和 full-train 排序的一致性。如果时间有限，优先看介绍实验主结果的图表、timeliness 相关消融、以及 strict vs broad filter 的能力权衡分析。值得深挖的点有三个：一是 timeliness 为什么会成为最强信号，它到底是在过滤新闻噪声，还是在提升知识稳定性与可复用推理模式；二是 taxonomy 标签误差对最终数据选择的鲁棒性；三是这种方法如何接到真实生产 pipeline，例如先用 composite score 做粗分层，再在低 tier 内做 taxonomy recovery，而不是完全替换现有系统。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a structural flaw in current web-data curation for pretraining: document value is treated as a single scalar quality score, while in practice it is multi-dimensional and capability-dependent. Standard pipelines rank documents with a composite scorer, then over-sample the top tiers and discard or downweight the rest. This collapses distinct properties—reasoning depth, educational value, temporal durability, cultural scope—into one opaque number. The result is systematic loss of documents that are weak on the aggregate score but strong on dimensions that matter for downstream model capabilities. The paper is not trying to build a slightly better quality ranker. It is trying to recover latent training value from deprioritized web corpora through interpretable, semantically grounded filtering.","importance":"For pretraining, the contribution is significant because it reframes data curation from scalar ranking to controllable capability-oriented selection. First, it shows that low- and mid-tier web data contain recoverable value that conventional quality pipelines miss. Second, it makes filtering more operational: instead of asking which documents are globally “good,” one can ask which documents are evergreen, reasoning-heavy, broad in knowledge, or suitable for coding. That is much closer to how training mixtures are actually optimized. Third, the work is engineered for scale rather than only for analysis. The authors annotate 14M documents with a large model, distill the labels into smaller annotators, and use a two-pass search procedure to avoid combinatorial explosion in compound filters. For researchers working on web pretraining, continual pretraining, or data remixing before mid-training, this is a practical data-selection framework rather than a taxonomy exercise.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"$18","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"数据筛选别只信单分数；建议复现它的维度标注和 two-pass 搜索，看是否能稳定改 pretrain mix。","en":"Do not treat one scalar data score as enough; the taxonomy labels and two-pass filter search deserve an internal pretraining ablation."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Neeraj Varshney","Sanket Lokegaonkar","Nasser Zalmout","Qingyu Yin","Priyanka Nigam","Bing Yin"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["data-curation","web-corpus","quality-filtering","taxonomy","pretraining-data"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07778","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.07778","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.07597","title":"Repetition Mismatch: Why Data Mixture Experiments Don't Scale and How to Fix Them","abstract":"Pre-training data mixtures are commonly tuned by running small-scale experiments and extrapolating to the target training budget. The contribution is not another generic scaling plot; it isolates a confound that has often been mixed into data-mixture tuning: repetition mismatch. That directly affects mixture transferability, compute efficiency, and how one should reason about the optimal share of scarce high-quality data at larger budgets.","technical_approach":"The authors argue that small-scale mixture sweeps fail mainly because the repetition rate of small high-quality datasets changes with training horizon, causing the optimal mixture to drift with budget. To control this variable, they propose repetition-controlled subsampling: in the proxy experiment, subsample data so that its repetition rate matches the one expected at the target budget. Detecting whether specific datasets were used during pretraining is, therefore, critical. Existing state-of-the-art methods typically rely on access to model probability distributions, making them unsuitable for closed-source LLMs that provide only input-output interfaces. To address this limitation, we int","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-06T06:27:54Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2606_07996.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是黑盒 LLM 的语料级预训练数据检测问题：在只能通过 API 输入输出交互、拿不到 token probability 和模型内部状态时，判断某个候选语料是否进入过模型预训练数据。","importance":"它对预训练研究的价值主要在数据审计和污染检测，而不是训练方法本身。现有 membership inference 往往依赖 likelihood、perplexity 或 token-level 概率，闭源模型通常不给这些信号。MC-PDD 把检测信号改成“模型能否补出高度特异的被遮蔽 token”，使得预训练数据检测可以在更严格的黑盒条件下执行。对基座研究员来说，启发点在于：记忆检测不一定只能看概率分布，也可以通过设计低可推断、高特异性的查询，把训练记忆转化为可观测的生成命中率。","technical_approach":"方法很直接。对候选 corpus 中每条文本，先用 TF-IDF 找出最具区分度、最不容易从上下文泛化推断的 token；然后把该 token mask 掉，提示 LLM 预测空缺内容；最后统计预测命中率，并和一个非成员参考语料的命中率做语料级显著性检验。关键设计是 corpus-level，而不是 sample-level。这样可以降低单条样本生成随机性、模型泛化能力、prompt 不稳定性带来的噪声。与依赖概率分布的白盒或灰盒方法相比，MC-PDD 只使用模型最终输出；与普通黑盒问答式检测相比，它通过 TF-IDF 选择“具体但难猜”的 token，试图减少模型凭语言能力补全答案的可能。","experiments":"论文在开放模型和闭源模型上做实验，并构造了 SteamMIA 与 arXivMIA-OLMo 两个检测数据集。SteamMIA 使用 Steam 游戏元数据，按时间构造 2014 与 2024 的成员/非成员候选；arXivMIA-OLMo 使用 2017 年 2 月和 2025 年 2 月的 arXiv 摘要，并结合 OLMo-7B-Instruct 的预训练数据可验证性来建立标签。实验报告显示，成员语料和非成员语料的预测 hit rate 存在统计显著差异；即使用约 100 条样本也能观察到差距。论文还在 BBC News 和 arXiv 数据上做鲁棒性测试，结论是 MC-PDD 在只依赖输入输出的设定下能达到有竞争力的检测效果。需要注意，核心指标是语料级命中率差异和显著性，而不是单样本 membership 判别。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看任务设定部分，确认它的黑盒约束比常见 likelihood-based 方法更严格；再看方法部分的 TF-IDF token 选择、mask prompt 设计、hit-rate 统计检验。实验部分建议关注三点：参考非成员语料如何选，100 samples 下的稳定性如何，模型泛化能力是否会造成假阳性。局限也要看清楚：如果被 mask 的 token 可以由上下文强推断，或者闭源模型有强安全策略、输出格式不稳定，检测信号会被削弱。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies corpus-level pretraining data detection for black-box LLMs: given only input-output API access, decide whether a candidate corpus was included in the model’s pretraining data.","importance":"Its main relevance to pretraining research is data auditing and contamination analysis, not a new training recipe. Many existing membership inference methods depend on token probabilities, likelihoods, or perplexity-like signals, which are unavailable for most closed-source models. MC-PDD reframes the detection signal as whether the model can recover highly specific masked tokens. This is useful because it turns memorization into an observable generation behavior under a strict black-box setting.","technical_approach":"MC-PDD selects a highly specific token from each text using TF-IDF, masks that token, prompts the LLM to fill in the blank, and measures exact prediction hits. The final decision is made at the corpus level by testing whether the average hit rate on the candidate corpus is significantly higher than that on a non-member reference corpus. The corpus-level design is important: individual generations are noisy, while aggregate hit-rate gaps are more stable. Compared with probability-based detectors, the method does not require logits or token probabilities. Compared with simpler prompting-based probes, the TF-IDF selection tries to choose tokens that are hard to infer from context alone, so a correct answer is more likely to reflect memorization.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the method on both open-source and closed-source models. It introduces SteamMIA, built from Steam game metadata around 2014 and 2024, and arXivMIA-OLMo, built from arXiv abstracts from February 2017 and February 2025 with labels tied to the OLMo-7B-Instruct pretraining data. The reported results show statistically significant hit-rate gaps between member and non-member corpora, even with around 100 samples. Additional robustness experiments are conducted on BBC News and arXiv-style data. The main reported signal is corpus-level hit-rate separation rather than reliable per-example membership classification.","reading_guide":"A skim read is sufficient, around 20–30 minutes. In this paper, we introduce PoE-Bridge, a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-06T08:21:06Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08048.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 diffusion language model 并行解码质量不足的问题：DLM 可以并行生成多个 token，但同一步内 token 之间缺少强依赖建模；如果直接用 AR 模型做 rejection sampling 或 importance sampling 校正，又会因为 DLM proposal 和 AR target 分布差距太大而计算成本过高。","importance":"它的价值在于把 diffusion LM 的并行解码和 AR LM 的条件评分能力接起来。对预训练研究员来说，这类工作不只是 serving 技巧，也会反过来影响建模范式的判断：如果 DLM 需要 AR 模型作为 verifier 或 target 才能接近高质量生成，那么 DLM 的优势更可能来自并行 proposal，而不是完全替代 AR 条件分布。PoE-Bridge 给出了一种中间分布校正思路，可以用来理解非 AR 语言模型与 AR 模型之间的分布差距。","technical_approach":"核心方法是引入一个 Product-of-Experts bridge distribution，作为 DLM proposal 和 AR target 之间的中间分布。直观形式是把 DLM 分布和 AR 分布按权重 w 做 PoE 插值；w 接近 1 时更靠近 DLM，w 接近 0 时更靠近 AR。解码被拆成两步：先用 DLM 并行产生候选 continuation，再用 speculative rejection sampling 把候选过滤到 PoE bridge 附近；随后用 importance resampling 从 bridge 进一步校正到 AR target。这样避免了直接从 DLM 到 AR 的大跨度校正。论文还加入两个工程机制：Mixed-Temperature Sampling 用不同温度生成候选，缓解 importance sampling 中候选塌缩和覆盖不足；Elastic Rejection Window 根据已观察到的接受长度动态调整验证窗口，减少固定窗口带来的无效验证计算。","experiments":"论文在数学推理和代码任务上评估 PoE-Bridge。报告结果显示，相比标准 DLM 解码，PoE-Bridge 在准确率上更高，同时最高有约 5 倍生成加速；相比目标 AR 解码，它能恢复至少 95% 的 AR 准确率，并达到约 2 倍吞吐。实验重点不是训练一个更强的 DLM，而是在给定 DLM proposal 和 AR target 的条件下，通过桥接分布降低 Monte Carlo 校正难度。消融上应重点关注 PoE 权重 w、candidate 数量、mixed-temperature 策略、elastic window 对有效样本数和接受率的影响。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 30–40 分钟。优先看方法中的 PoE bridge 定义，尤其是它如何从序列级概念落到可计算的 token-level 形式；再看 speculative rejection sampling 与 importance resampling 的衔接，因为这是方法有效的关键。实验部分建议看数学和代码任务上的 accuracy-throughput tradeoff，以及 candidate budget 较小时是否仍然稳定。若时间有限，先读 Section 4.2、解码算法伪代码、主结果表和消融表。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses the quality gap in parallel decoding for diffusion language models. DLMs can generate multiple tokens in parallel, but their within-step token independence weakens generation quality. Directly correcting DLM samples with an AR model through rejection sampling or importance sampling is expensive because the DLM proposal and AR target distributions are far apart.","importance":"The contribution is a decoding framework that combines DLM parallel proposal generation with AR conditional scoring. For pretraining researchers, the result is useful because it clarifies where current DLMs help and where they still rely on AR structure. The DLM is effective as a parallel proposal, while the AR model supplies a stronger dependency-aware target. The bridge distribution is also a useful lens for measuring and reducing the distribution mismatch between non-AR language models and AR models.","technical_approach":"PoE-Bridge introduces an intermediate Product-of-Experts distribution between the DLM proposal and the AR target. Conceptually, the bridge interpolates the two distributions with a weight w: when w is close to 1, it stays closer to the DLM; when w is close to 0, it approaches the AR target. Decoding is decomposed into two easier transitions. First, DLM candidates are generated in parallel and filtered toward the PoE bridge using speculative rejection sampling. Second, importance resampling refines the filtered candidates toward the AR target. The paper adds two practical mechanisms: Mixed-Temperature Sampling, which generates candidates at different entropy levels to improve coverage and reduce sample collapse; and Elastic Rejection Window, which adapts the verification window based on observed acceptance lengths to avoid wasted verification compute.","experiments":"The paper evaluates PoE-Bridge on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks. It reports higher accuracy than standard DLM decoding with up to about 5x speedup over that DLM decoding setup, and it recovers at least 95% of the target AR model’s accuracy while reaching around 2x the throughput of AR decoding. The key experimental question is not whether the DLM alone is stronger, but whether the bridge reduces the Monte Carlo correction burden under realistic candidate budgets. The most informative ablations are likely the PoE weight, number of particles or candidates, mixed-temperature sampling, elastic rejection window, acceptance length, and effective sample size.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take 30–40 minutes. Start with the formal definition of the PoE bridge, especially how the sequence-level idea is implemented in a tractable token-level form. Then read the decoding algorithm to understand how speculative rejection sampling and importance resampling are composed. In the experiments, focus on the accuracy-throughput frontier for math and coding tasks, and check whether the method remains stable with small candidate budgets. If time is limited, prioritize Section 4.2, the algorithm box, the main result table, and the ablations.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读方法和采样分析即可；它不改变 DLM 训练目标，但对“DLM 如何借 AR verifier 补依赖”给了一个干净接口。","en":"Read the method and sampling analysis; it does not change DLM training, but gives a clean interface for using an AR verifier to restore dependencies."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Stanford University"],"authors":["Juntong Shi","Brian L. We propose CHIAR-Former (Chiaroscuro Attention), a 4-layer hybrid transformer that routes each token to one of three operators - DCT spectral mixing, RBF kernel mixing, or full self-attention - based on per-token spectral entropy, a theoretically justified complexity signal. Through systematic ablation on WikiText-103, we discover routing collap","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-06T20:38:42Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08327.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作想解决的是 Transformer 在所有 token、所有层上一视同仁地做 full self-attention 所带来的计算浪费。作者的判断是，不同 token 的跨 token 交互需求并不相同：有些 token 结构平滑、局部可预测，不值得付出 O(n^2 d) 的代价；只有少数高复杂度 token 真正需要动态全局交互。核心问题因此不是再做一种统一的 attention 近似，而是能否给每个 token 一个有理论依据的复杂度信号，再据此在层内切换不同 operator。","importance":"对 LLM 基座研究的直接价值有限，更多属于架构探索和高风险原型，而不是可立即进入大规模预训练 recipe 的方案。它有启发的地方在于两点。第一，它把 token-adaptive compute 从“跳层/跳 token”推进到“按 token 选择不同 mixing operator”，这比单纯稀疏化更接近条件计算的完整形态。第二，论文里最有信息量的发现不是三路 routing 本身，而是 routing collapse：RBF 分支在训练中被系统性淘汰，只剩 DCT 和 attention 形成互补。这说明在语言建模里，便宜的全局频域混合和昂贵的动态内容交互之间，可能已经覆盖了大部分有用归纳偏置，中间那类局部核混合未必值得保留。","technical_approach":"模型叫 CHIAR-Former，是一个 4 层 hybrid transformer。每个 token 在某层会根据 spectral entropy 被路由到三种 operator 之一：DCT spectral mixing、RBF kernel mixing、或 full self-attention。spectral entropy 来自 token representation 在离散余弦变换后的功率谱分布。若能量集中在少数低频，熵低，说明表示较平滑，可用 DCT 做便宜混合；若频谱分散，熵高，说明 token 更复杂，送去 attention；中间区间原本交给 RBF kernel mixing，用随机傅里叶特征近似核映射。\n\n作者给了一个理论框架：低熵 token 的 DCT 截断误差可由谱尾能量界定；中等熵 token 适合 Mercer kernel 近似；高熵 token 则需要动态 cross-token projection，也就是 attention。和 FNet 这类“全局替换 attention 为频域混合”的方法不同，这里是 selective use；和 Mixture-of-Depths、LayerSkip 不同，这里不跳过计算，而是保留层深度、切换 operator 类型。全文真正重要的技术结论是 learned routing collapse。训练后 router 持续拒绝 RBF，偏向 DCT+Attention。于是作者又构造了一个 purpose-designed 的 DCT+Attention-only 变体，直接删掉 RBF，把 collapse 从训练现象变成架构选择。","experiments":"论文最醒目的数字是 WikiText-103 上的验证集 perplexity：DCT+Attention 变体达到 36.54，而 full-attention baseline 是 66.6，按文中说法是 45% 的 PPL 改善，同时 attention FLOPs 降低 62.5%。如果这个 baseline 设置公平，这个幅度相当大；但也正因为幅度过大，读实验时需要特别核对 baseline 容量、训练步数、优化器、上下文长度、参数量是否完全对齐。全文还说在四个数据集上做了评测，并观察到明显的 operating regime：在大规模、自然文本分布上收益更明显；在小数据集和 synthetic pattern-matching 任务上，full attention 反而更稳。这一点比单个最好数字更可信，也更有研究价值。\n\n消融方面，最值得看的是三类：一是三路 routing 与两路 DCT+Attention 的比较，用来验证 RBF 是否真的无贡献；二是不同 entropy threshold 的敏感性；三是 collapse 现象是否稳定出现在不同随机种子和数据集上。如果 collapse 只在单一设置出现，结论会弱很多。文中还把方法与 FNet、MoE routing、Mamba、FlashAttention、GQA 做了定位，但这些更多是概念对照，不是严格 apples-to-apples 的主实验。","reading_guide":"这篇适合带着怀疑去读。先看 Introduction 和 Theoretical Framework，确认 spectral entropy 的定义、阈值划分、以及作者为何认为它能代表 token complexity。然后直接跳到实验和 ablation，重点看三件事：baseline 是否公平、routing collapse 是否稳定、以及收益是否主要来自 selective attention 还是来自额外的 spectral mixing inductive bias。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看 applying full self-attention uniformly to every token at every layer. The authors argue that tokens differ in how much dynamic cross-token interaction they need. Some are smooth and locally predictable, while others require expensive global content-dependent mixing. The core problem is therefore not just to approximate attention globally, but to find a principled per-token complexity signal and use it to switch among operators inside a layer.","importance":"Its direct relevance to large-scale LLM pretraining is limited for now. Gaussian perturbations work naturally in continuous domains because the space has a meaningful local geometry. Token spaces do not. Masking, random replacement, absorbing states, or hand-crafted transition kernels impose artificial intermediate distributions. The reverse model is trained to invert those distributions, but generation-time drafts contain model-specific errors that need not resemble the prescribed noising process.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is the shift from designing a corruption process to learning recursive distribution refinement. This is relevant to masked diffusion LMs, SEDD-like models, MDLM, and LLaDA-style systems. The paper suggests that discrete language diffusion may not need a token-level neighborhood model; intermediate states can instead be induced by the model's own drafts. The contribution is at the modeling-objective and train-inference-alignment level, not just a sampler modification. The evidence is still limited to a 4B model with 10B tokens of continued pretraining, so the scaling behavior remains open.","technical_approach":"FReDA removes the hand-designed forward process and formulates diffusion language modeling as recursive distribution refinement. A model produces draft sequences, and the learned transition improves those drafts toward the data distribution. The implicit intermediate states are generated by premature or current versions of the model rather than by an externally specified noise schedule. The paper instantiates this idea in two ways. Self-refinement directly revises the model's own draft. Stochastic best-of-N refinement samples multiple candidate sequences in parallel and uses an additional scoring head to select the most promising one. This differs from D3PM or absorbing-mask diffusion, where q(x_t|x_0) is specified first and the model learns its reverse. In FReDA, the sampler induces the distributional path, and the difficulty of each refinement step adapts to the model's expressive capacity.","experiments":"The reported model is FReDA-4B trained with a 10B-token continued-pretraining corpus, smaller than the budgets used by several diffusion baselines. Evaluation covers general knowledge, mathematical reasoning, and code generation in the sub-8B regime. FReDA-4B outperforms 7B-8B diffusion base models on four reasoning and coding benchmarks, with absolute gains of roughly 5-15%, while remaining competitive on general knowledge tasks. On efficiency, it reaches a stronger quality-speed trade-off: fewer forward passes are needed for comparable accuracy, more tokens are produced per forward pass, and the average speedup is reported as 1.5-1.8x. Additional analyses show consistent gains as refinement computation increases.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30-45 minutes. Start with the Introduction, especially the argument against fixed corruption paths in discrete token space. Then read the formal definition of recursive distribution refinement and compare it with methods that explicitly define q(x_t|x_0). The two implementation choices, self-refinement and stochastic best-of-N refinement, are the key method details; the training and usage of the score head deserve attention. In the experiments, focus on four points: the 4B/10B-token setup, fairness against LLaDA/MDLM-like 7B-8B diffusion baselines, refinement-iteration ablations, and the quality-speed curve. misali","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-07T07:59:55Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08501.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"PAPO 解决的是 diffusion LLM 做 RL 训练时的轨迹错位问题。现有方法常把最终答案 reward 粗略分配给任意中间去噪步，并用随机 mask 最终答案构造训练状态；这些状态不一定来自真实生成轨迹，导致 credit assignment 粗、梯度利用低，推理和训练过程不一致。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练研究的价值在于，它把 RL 信号如何接入非自回归语言模型讲得更具体。AR 模型的 policy trajectory 天然是 token-by-token，GRPO/PPO 类方法可以直接沿生成序列分配 log-prob 和 reward。masked diffusion LM 的生成过程是多步去噪和重 mask，训练状态不是线性 token 前缀，因此不能直接照搬 AR RL。PAPO 的贡献是把 reward 和 update state 都对齐到真实 denoising trajectory，这对 LLaDA、MDLM、SEDD 一类模型后训练和 reasoning tuning 都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"PAPO 包含两个核心组件。\n\n第一是 Step-Aware Process Rewards，简称 SPR。它不把最终 outcome reward 直接广播给所有中间步，而是基于单步 denoising prediction 为每个中间步骤计算即时 reward，再与最终答案 reward 组合成训练信号。这样做的目标是区分哪些去噪步骤真正改善了答案，哪些步骤只是被最终正确答案“顺带奖励”。对于 diffusion LM，这一点很关键，因为一次 denoising 可能同时改变多个 token，错误来源不一定能按 AR 的 token 顺序拆开。\n\n第二是 Entropy-Guided Historical Re-enactment，简称 EHR。它不再通过随机 mask 最终 completion 来构造人工状态，而是记录自然 rollout 过程中出现过的真实历史状态，并在训练时重放这些状态。选择哪些状态更新时，EHR 用 entropy 作为采样信号，优先训练模型不确定性高的步骤，少把梯度花在已经稳定的 token 或低信息状态上。\n\n整体上，PAPO 可以看作是面向 masked diffusion LM 的 GRPO 式改造：保留 group-relative advantage 的思想，但把 log-prob、reward 和状态采样都重新绑定到 denoising 轨迹上。","experiments":"实验覆盖数学推理和规划任务。数学任务包括 GSM8K 和 MATH500，PAPO 相对已有 barrier in diffusion language model decoding. In common block-wise semi-autoregressive decoding, the next block cannot start until the current block is fully decoded or its denoising budget is exhausted. This serializes two different operations: refining low-confidence positions in the current block and advancing the generation frontier. Once a stable prefix or delimiter boundary has emerged, the continuation does not always need to wait for every remaining token in the current block to be resolved.","importance":"The direct relevance to pretraining is moderate; this is primarily a decoding and serving scheduler for diffusion LMs. Its broader lesson is useful: the throughput advantage of non-AR models depends not only on predicting multiple masked tokens per pass, but also on how the inference dependency graph is scheduled. It also gives a modeling signal. If a diffusion or any-order LM can expose reliable boundaries early, decoding can exploit more parallelism. The work therefore sits between modeling assumptions and systems efficiency.","technical_approach":"AsyncLane is a training-free scheduler. It replaces the single global block pointer in standard block-wise decoding with a set of active lanes. A generate lane advances the frontier. A refine lane keeps improving an already discovered prefix. When a generate lane reveals a reliable delimiter boundary, the scheduler performs branch-and-refine: the prefix before the boundary is assigned to a refine lane, and the continuation after the boundary is assigned to a new generate lane. The future region can then begin denoising while the past remains editable. To keep the cost under control, AsyncLane exploits shared prefix states among forked sibling lanes and evaluates them with grouped batched forwards. It reuses future-window logits as high-confidence drafts for later generate lanes, uses cascading termination to avoid creating lanes beyond EOS or unresolved frontiers, and applies compact cache refresh plus refresh-logit reuse to reduce stale-prefix issues under bidirectional attention.","experiments":"Experiments are reported on LLaDA and Dream backbones, with mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks. AsyncLane achieves the highest throughput across the evaluated benchmark-length settings, and the advantage becomes larger for longer generations. Generation quality remains competitive, so the speedup is not simply obtained by allowing large quality degradation. Progress in both paradigms is overwhelmingly tracked by generative perplexity (gen-PPL): the per-token negative log-likelihood of samples under a frozen autoregressive (AR) scorer such as gpt2-large, typically paired with an empirical-entropy guardrail to rule out low-entropy collapse. We argue that this metric is unsound. By construction, gen-PPL measures only pr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-07T02:35:56Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08417.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“如何训练更好的非自回归语言模型”，而是一个更基础的问题：当前领域里用来比较 diffusion / flow / 其他非 AR 文本生成模型的主指标 gen-PPL，是否真的在衡量生成质量。全文给出的结论是否定的。gen-PPL 的定义是：把生成样本交给一个冻结的 AR scorer（通常是 GPT-2 large），计算其每 token 负对数似然。这个量只反映“样本对该 scorer 来说有多可预测”，并不直接反映语法完整性、语义连贯性、篇章一致性，更不反映样本分布是否接近人类文本。论文要解决的核心问题，就是证明这种错位不是边角问题，而是结构性缺陷：存在大量低质量、甚至明显胡乱的文本，在冻结 AR scorer 下依然能拿到很低的 gen-PPL；因此用它来追踪非 AR LM 的进展，会系统性误排方法优劣。","importance":"对预训练研究的影响主要有两层。第一层是评测层面。近两年 diffusion LM、masked/any-order LM、continuous flow LM 的论文，很多都把 gen-PPL 当成主结果表。若这个指标可以被零参数、刻意构造的劣质采样器“刷分”，那么已有结论里相当一部分只能说明“更像 scorer 偏好的可预测串”，不能说明“更接近真实文本分布”。第二层是研究方向层面。非 AR 语言建模一直面临 exact likelihood 难算、采样策略多样、推断步数影响结果等问题，社区因此依赖代理指标。本文的贡献在于把这个代理指标的失效机制讲清楚，并给出替代思路：不要再问样本在某个 AR 模型下是否容易预测，而要问生成分布与人类文本分布之间的统计距离。对做新型语言建模范式的人，这篇文章的价值不在于提出一个新模型，而在于纠正“优化错目标”的风险，量级上属于会影响后续实验 protocol 的工作。","technical_approach":"技术路线分三步。第一步是理论和概念拆解。作者把 gen-PPL 重新解释为“冻结 AR scorer 对样本序列的条件可预测性”，并指出这与生成质量不是同一个对象。一个强 AR scorer 会偏好高频局部模式、常见搭配、短程可预测结构；但文本质量还包含长程语义、全局一致性、信息密度、 discourse 合理性。只要能构造出在局部上高度可预测、在全局上却很差的序列，gen-PPL 就会失真。经验熵 guardrail 也不够，因为 unigram entropy 只能排除最粗糙的低熵塌缩，挡不住“词表使用看起来够丰富，但组合方式很差”的样本。\n\n第二步是构造反例生成器。文章的关键说服力来自一组 zero-parameter、deliberately naive samplers。它们不训练模型，不学习数据分布，只利用简单启发式或 scorer 偏好，就能生成明显不自然的文本，同时取得很强的 gen-PPL。这里的重点不是某个具体 sampler 的工程细节，而是作者系统展示了：只要朝“提高 scorer 可预测性”这个方向做手工设计，就能稳定骗过 gen-PPL。这说明问题不在某个模型族，而在指标定义本身。全文反复强调，这些采样器输出在人工阅读下是 incoherent 的，但在 LM1B 和 OpenWebText 上却能达到或超过近期 diffusion / flow 模型报告的 gen-PPL。\n\n第三步是替代评测协议。作者主张把核心 proxy 从单点评分器的 token-level NLL，换成 generated distribution 与 reference distribution 的统计距离。文中推荐直接使用 distributional metrics，引用了 MMD、energy distance 等两样本检验/分布距离工具，并据此重新 benchmark 近期非 AR 模型。这个思路的关键不是某一个距离公式，而是评测对象从“单条样本在 scorer 下的分数”转向“样本集合是否像来自同一分布”。这更接近 unconditional generation 的真实目标，也更不容易被局部可预测性投机利用。","experiments":"实验部分最重要的结果有三类。第一类是反例成立：作者在 LM1B 和 OpenWebText 上，用零参数 naive samplers 取得了与近期非 AR 文献相当甚至更好的 gen-PPL，同时保持非退化的 empirical entropy。也就是说，它们并不是靠重复单词或极端低熵塌缩来刷分，而是在 entropy guardrail 之内完成“作弊”。摘要截断处没有给出完整数值表，但从正文叙述可以确定，作者把这些结果直接与已发表 diffusion-based 和 continuous-flow 模型做了并列比较，结论是 gen-PPL 排名会把明显更差的文本排到更前面。\n\n第二类是人类可感知质量与 gen-PPL 脱钩。论文通过样例和定量比较说明，低 gen-PPL 并不对应更好的语义连贯性。换句话说，指标排序与人工直觉排序发生了系统偏离，而不是偶然噪声。这个点很关键，因为如果只是少量异常样本，问题还可以归因于 scorer 偶发偏差；但作者展示的是组合空间里存在大量此类序列。\n\n第三类是重评测结果。作者用 distributional metrics 重新评估近期非 AR 模型后，得到的进展图景与 gen-PPL 下的结论不同。哪些模型真正更接近人类文本分布，哪些只是更会迎合 AR scorer，在新协议下被重新区分开。对读者来说，最值得看的不是某个模型绝对名次，而是“同一批模型在两套指标下排序如何变化”。这直接揭示了旧指标的误导程度。\n\n如果从实验设计角度看，文章还有两个细节值得记住：一是数据集选了 LM1B 和 OpenWebText，覆盖较规范与较开放的文本分布；二是作者没有只批评指标，而是给出可复现的对照基线和替代 protocol，这让结论更扎实。","reading_guide":"建议先读 Introduction 和作者对 gen-PPL 的问题定义，确认他们批评的不是“AR scorer 不够强”，而是“指标与目标错位”。然后重点看构造 naive samplers 的章节，以及展示样本文本的图表或案例。这里最能建立直觉：为什么这些文本读起来差，但 scorer 仍给高分。接着看 re-benchmark 部分，关注同一组模型在 gen-PPL 与 distributional metrics 下的排序变化。若时间有限，优先看三类内容：1）gen-PPL 与 empirical entropy 的定义和局限；2）零参数反例的构造逻辑；3）推荐的新评测协议及其结果。\n\n值得深挖的点有三个。第一，distributional metrics 在文本空间里依赖什么表征，是否会引入新的 representation bias；第二，两样本距离对 sample size、长度分布、解码温度是否敏感；第三，这套批评是否同样适用于一些常见的 AR 生成评测代理，比如用更强 judge LM 打分。做新型 LM 的人可以把这篇文章当成“评测 sanity check 清单”。在自己报结果前，先问一句：指标到底在奖励什么。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a basic but underexamined issue in non-autoregressive text generation: whether generative perplexity (gen-PPL) is a valid proxy for unconditional generation quality. The answer developed through the full paper is no. Gen-PPL evaluates generated samples under a frozen autoregressive scorer, usually GPT-2 large, and reports token-level negative log-likelihood. That quantity measures how predictable a sample is to the scorer, not whether the sample is grammatical, coherent, semantically meaningful, or distributionally close to human text. The central problem is therefore not a small mismatch but a structural one: there exists a large combinatorial set of low-quality sequences that are still highly predictable under the scorer, so progress measured by gen-PPL can be badly misranked.","importance":"Its importance is mainly methodological. A large fraction of recent diffusion LM, masked/any-order LM, and continuous flow LM work has relied on gen-PPL as the headline metric because exact likelihood is hard or intractable under these paradigms. If a metric can be beaten by zero-parameter, intentionally naive samplers that produce visibly poor text, then many published comparisons only show better compatibility with the scorer’s local preferences, not better language modeling. For pretraining research, this matters because evaluation shapes optimization pressure. Once a field starts tuning architectures, samplers, and decoding schedules against a flawed proxy, it can drift toward the wrong target. We discover they are not: agents are most vulnerable at the very start of a session and become substantially safer after a few regular agentic tasks -- a phenomenon we term the cold-start safety gap. To study this systematically, we introduce Safety Over Depth for Agents (SODA), a benchmark that controls how many regular agentic tasks the agent completes before encountering a safety threat, supporting up to 20 preceding tasks. E","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-05T21:58:19Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_07867.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"工具调用型 LLM agent 的安全性是否随对话进程变化；如果会变化，为什么“会话刚开始”更容易被诱导执行有害工具操作，以及如何用最小代价缓解。","importance":"这是偏评测+机制分析的工作，但对预训练/对齐闭环有直接启发：同一个已对齐模型，其安全行为不是静态属性，而是强依赖“上下文深度/历史轨迹”的动态状态。它提示两类训练与评测缺口：\n- 评测缺口：只测 D=0 的单轮威胁会系统性高估或低估真实部署风险（文中显示 D=0 最危险）。\n- 训练缺口：对齐可能更像一种可被上下文激活的“persona/模式”，而不是始终在线的硬约束；这会影响 pretrain→posttrain 的接口设计（system prompt 不足以稳定激活 agent 安全模式）。","technical_approach":"核心贡献是 SODA 基准与对“冷启动安全缺口”的归因。\n1) SODA（Safety Over Depth for Agents）：用“深度可控的威胁注入”设计，把同一类安全威胁放在不同对话深度 D 下评测。D 定义为威胁出现前，agent 已完成的常规工具任务数量，支持最高 20 个前置任务。作者构造 16 个工具环境（如 BankAccount、EmailManager、FileSystem、WebBrowser 等），每个环境 5 个场景（共 80），每个场景 5 个安全威胁（共 400 threats）。前置任务从同场景的“常规任务池”随机采样，确保工具分布与交互形态接近真实 agent 使用。\n2) 现象：安全性随 D 增大而单调提升，提出 cold-start safety gap。\n3) 机制与消融：把“历史里有什么”拆开。\n- 结论一：主要驱动来自“用户侧的常规 agent 任务请求”出现在历史里，而不是 agent 自己先前输出的具体内容；把 agent 回复替换成简短同意文本或随机文本，安全提升仍接近。\n- 结论二：但要保持 utility，真实的 agent 回复/工具交互过程很关键；用假回复会伤害工具使用能力。\n4) 表征分析：用隐藏状态做线性可分的 safety boundary，观察随深度增加，hidden states 向“更安全区域”迁移，支持“逐步激活 agent persona”的解释。\n5) 缓解策略：warm-up——在处理潜在高风险请求前，先让 agent 做 5–10 个常规工具任务。","experiments":"模型：7 个模型，来自 4 个家族（Llama、Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Gemma）。\nSODA 主结果：当深度从 D=0 增加到 D=20，安全性提升 9%–52%（不同模型幅度不同）。\n泛化：warm-up 效果迁移到其他开源 agent 安全基准：AgentHarm 安全 +23%，Agent Safety Bench（ASB）+8%。\n效用评估：在 BFCL、API-Bank 上验证 warm-up 不损害工具调用效用；同时消融显示“替换 agent 回复为无关文本”会降低效用（即安全提升不依赖回复内容，但效用依赖真实交互轨迹）。\n机制证据：表示空间随深度跨过线性 safety 边界（需要结合正文图/附录看具体层、具体表示取法：last token/mean pooling/某层等）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 35–50 分钟）：\n- 先看 SODA 的构造细节：16 个环境的工具 框架。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Whether tool-using LLM agents are equally safe throughout a session; why they are most vulnerable at the very beginning (cold start), and how to We argue this discriminative gap reflects an objective mismatch: self-generated rubrics describe good responses, whereas effective criteria must discriminate between close candidates. To close this gap, we introduce SVR (Support Vector Rubrics), a framework that recasts rubric construction as max-margin boundary learning over p","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-06T09:55:22Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08077.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 LLM judge 里一个很具体但很关键的问题：自动生成 rubric 时，模型往往写出的是“好答案通常应具备什么”，而不是“这两个候选答案究竟差在哪里”。前者偏描述性，后者才是偏好学习和奖励建模真正需要的判别性标准。论文把 RubricBench 上自生成 rubric 与人工 rubric 之间二十多个点的准确率差距，解释为目标错配而不是 judge 本身不会判断。要解决的核心问题因此不是提升 judge 的语言能力，而是如何从 preference data 中学到一套靠近决策边界、能稳定迁移、且在推理时不依赖候选答案内容的 rubric 机制。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改 pretrain recipe，但和 pretrain↔post-train 接口关系很紧。原因有两点。第一，偏好数据的监督信号质量会直接影响 reward model、RLHF、RLAIF 乃至后续合成数据的质量，而 rubric 是把偏好信号结构化的一种方式。第二，这篇工作给出一个很清楚的判断：评估标准如果由候选答案反向诱导出来，就容易被表面风格和局部措辞牵着走；如果标准先固定，再去打分，信号会更稳。这对 reward hacking、judge overfitting、以及训练时如何构造更可验证的反馈，都有直接启发。贡献量级上，它更像是“把 rubric generation 从 prompt-time heuristic 变成可训练的判别模块”，不是小修小补。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是把 rubric bank 当成一个全局、可复用的判别特征空间。训练数据是标准 preference triples (x, r+, r-)。系统先做 contrastive feature induction：不是问模型“好回答应满足什么”，而是问“哪条 rubric 能把这对回答区分开”。这样得到的候选 rubric 会更贴近边界案例。随后构建全局 rubric bank，并对每条 rubric 配一个全局权重 w，同时学习一个仅依赖 prompt x 的 selector α(x)，从 bank 中选出 top-k rubric。这里有两个设计很关键。其一，selector 在推理时只看 prompt，不看待比较的回答，因此 rubric 不会被候选答案内容污染；其二，最终 pairwise margin 是多个 rubric 下局部判断的加权聚合，目标是让 F(x, r+, r-) > 0，并通过 max-margin 训练把注意力集中到难例上。\n\n论文进一步引入 support-pair 驱动的迭代 refinement。训练后，靠近 margin 或被错分的 preference pair 被视为 support pairs；系统再围绕这些难例做 adversarial probing，合成更难的 negative，暴露当前 rubric bank 没覆盖到的失败模式；然后再次做 contrastive induction，把新 failure mode 对应的 rubric 加入 bank。最后再按低权重、低支持度、冗余相似度做 pruning。这个流程和 SVM 的类比是成立的：不是让所有样本平均决定标准，而是让边界附近的样本塑造标准。和已有 prompt-only self-rubric 方法相比，区别不在 judge backbone，而在 rubric 的来源、训练目标和推理时机都变了。","experiments":"最关键的结果在 RubricBench。SVR 达到 82.8 的 pairwise accuracy，距离人工 rubric oracle 只差 0.3，说明它几乎把“自生成 rubric 与人工 rubric 的判别差距”补平了。论文强调这个提升是在 judge 模型不变的前提下得到的，因此增益主要来自 rubric 质量，而不是更强的 judge。除此之外，SVR 在 RewardBench 1/2 和 RM-Bench 上也保持竞争力，说明它没有为了 RubricBench 这种细粒度难例而牺牲一般偏好评测。\n\n更值得看的是迁移性和消融。全文给出的主张是：rubric bank 训练一次后，可以迁移到不同 judge family 和未见过的 preference benchmark。若这一点在表格里成立，它意味着 rubric 作为“判别标准层”比具体 judge backbone 更稳定。消融上，几个部件应当是主要增益来源：contrastive 而非 descriptive 的 rubric induction、prompt-conditioned selector、support-pair refinement、以及 adversarial hard negative 扩展。若去掉这些模块，性能会明显回落，尤其是在 RubricBench 这类专门过滤掉表面偏差、保留细微差别的数据上。论文还反复强调 bank pruning，不只是为了效率，也是在减少重叠 rubric 的重复计分问题。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，把“descriptive vs. discriminative rubric”这个问题意识建立起来；这是全文最重要的判断。然后看方法部分里四个组件：global rubric bank、prompt-conditioned selector、support-pair refinement、bank pruning。真正值得细读的是 support-pair 和 adversarial probing，因为这决定了它不是一次性做 prompt engineering，而是在 preference boundary 上持续补 rubric。\n\n实验部分建议重点看三类表：RubricBench 主结果、跨 judge/跨 benchmark 迁移、以及消融。读的时候要特别留意两件事。第一，性能提升究竟来自 rubric bank 本身，还是来自更强的 judge call budget；第二，top-k rubric 的数量、bank 大小、pruning 阈值这些超参对稳定性影响多大。预计 25–35 分钟可以抓住主线；如果要判断它能否进入 RLHF pipeline，建议再仔细看 inference protocol 和是否存在测试时信息泄漏。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in rubric-based LLM evaluation: self-generated rubrics tend to describe what a good answer should look like, but preference learning needs criteria that separate two close candidates near the decision boundary. The authors argue that the large gap between self-generated and human-written rubrics on RubricBench is not mainly a judge-capability issue. It is an objective mismatch. The real problem is how to learn reusable, discriminative rubrics from preference data, especially for hard pairs, while avoiding response-conditioned rubric generation at inference time.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. Preference optimization, reward modeling, and RLHF all depend on the quality of supervision signals. SVR suggests that a large part of judge unreliability comes from criterion construction rather than scoring ability. That is useful for anyone thinking about reward hacking, verifier design, or how to turn noisy pairwise preferences into more structured and stable training signals. The contribution is meaningful because it turns rubric generation from a prompt-time heuristic into a trainable discriminative layer with transfer across judges and benchmarks.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the main motivating figure. The descriptive-versus-discriminative distinction is the conceptual center of the paper. Then read the method in the order the system is built: global rubric bank, prompt-conditioned selector, support-pair refinement, and pruning. The support-pair and adversarial probing sections deserve the closest attention, since they determine whether this is a real learning framework rather than prompt engineering with extra steps.\n\nIn the experiments, focus on three blocks: RubricBench main results, transfer across judges and benchmarks, and ablations. Although well-studied in English, its manifestation in other languages remains largely unexamined, leaving billions of non-English speakers potentially vulnerable to model-validated misinformation. mil","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-07T04:50:40Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08451.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究多语言场景下的 network -- coupling frame-synchronous acoustic alignment with the LLM's li","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-07T07:15:34Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_08486.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"TRADE 解决的是 Speech LLM 的流式解码问题。现有 Speech LLM 多数按文本 token 自回归生成，缺少声学帧到文本 token 的显式对齐，因此很难自然支持低延迟流式识别、端点检测和长音频稳定转写。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练研究的启发在于，它把语音 LM 的“语言建模能力”和传统 transducer 的“帧同步对齐能力”做了更紧的结构耦合。它不是简单给 Speech LLM 加一个 CTC 辅助 loss，也不是靠固定 chunk 策略硬切流式输入，而是让 transducer 分支直接使用 LLM hidden states 作为 prediction network。这个设计说明，语音大模型要真正进入实时交互场景，不能只依赖更大的 decoder 或更多语音指令数据，还需要把声学时间结构显式放回生成机制里。","technical_approach":"TRADE 将 Hybrid TAED 的思想扩展到 decoder-only Speech LLM。模型使用共享的 Conformer audio encoder，一路送入 LLM，另一路进入 transducer。关键点是 transducer 的 prediction network 不再是独立小网络，而是直接使用 LLM 的 hidden states，因此 acoustic alignment 和 linguistic context 在同一个解码状态上发生作用。推理时，transducer 是主解码器，决定当前步是发 blank、推进声学帧，还是输出 token；LLM 提供更强的语言先验和全词表覆盖。\n\n论文还设计了 tightly coupled dual vocabularies。transducer 使用从 LLM 词表派生出的紧凑词表，保留原 token ID，并合并发音等价的 surface forms，降低 transducer lattice 的复杂度。推理时，LLM 对同音集合的概率质量做 marginalization，再与 transducer 分数融合。这样可以让 transducer 负责声学上更稳的发音级决策，同时让 LLM 恢复大小写、标点、拼写变体等表层形式。\n\n训练侧采用 joint transducer loss 和 LLM cross-entropy loss。为了解决流式训练和流式推理不一致的问题，TRADE 使用 dynamic chunk-based synchronized training，在 chunk 边界重新 prefill LLM 状态，使训练时看到的上下文更接近推理时的状态。为了控制显存，论文在 LLM 边界做 gradient stopping，使峰值内存接近离线训练量级。长音频方面，Localized Decoder Audio Attention 使用因果滑动窗口限制 LLM 对音频侧的注意力范围，避免 utterance 变长时 KV-cache 持续增长，也减少早期过时声学上下文对后续对齐的干扰。","experiments":"实验围绕四类能力展开：识别准确率、流式延迟-准确率权衡、长音频转写和端点检测。论文报告 TRADE 在 Open ASR Leaderboard 上取得有竞争力的识别效果；同一个 checkpoint 可以在多个 latency operating points 下工作，不需要为不同延迟单独训练；长音频转写可以依靠 frame-synchronous streaming decoder 原生完成，不需要外部 VAD 切段或 chunked batching；端点检测通过融合 transducer 的标点发射和 acoustic VAD 信号得到改善。给定内容没有包含完整 WER 表格和各 benchmark 指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TRADE targets the missing streaming mechanism in Speech LLMs. A standard Speech LLM decodes text tokens in a label-synchronous manner, without an learning；随后 RLHF、SFT、RLVR 等 post-training 逐渐扩大，直到消耗数百亿到数千亿 token。实验部分试图隔离预训练的作用。作者比较两类模型：一类是正常预训练模型，另一类是完全随机初始化、只在现代 reasoning SFT 数据上训练的模型。如果 post-training 只是释放预训练中已有的知识，随机初始化模型应当几乎失败；如果
